      this.spawnAlpha = Math.max(0, this.spawnAlpha - 1);
      if (this.spawnAlpha === 0) this.alphaFade = false;
    }

    const player = (game as unknown as { player: Player }).player;
    const hasPlayer = !!player && !player.dead;

    // ---- 原版数据驱动路径：按 aiStyle 分发到已移植的 AI 家族（分发后落入共享尾段） ----
    // 蠕虫身体段（wormFollow 非空）无 AI：位置由头部 wormAI 沿链驱动，但仍走共享尾段（接触伤害等）
    // 例外：EoW 三段各段独立跑 eowAI（断链自查 + 身/尾自跟随，:51874/:52266），
    // 不再由头统一驱动链——断链拆分后每条子虫的头各自驱动
    const eowSeg = this.vanillaId !== null && this.vanillaId >= 13 && this.vanillaId <= 15;
    const wormBody = this.wormFollow && !eowSeg;
    // 小动物（critter）优先走被动游荡，绝不落入 default 的追击 fallback
    if (this.vanilla && !wormBody && this.vanilla.critter) {
      this.critterWanderAI(game, hasPlayer ? player : null);
    } else if (this.vanilla && !wormBody) {
      const p = hasPlayer ? player : null;
      switch (this.vanilla.aiStyle) {
        case 92: this.dummyAI(game); break;                            // AI_092 训练假人（NPC 488）
        case 1: this.slimeAI(game, p); break;                          // AI_001 史莱姆
        case 2: this.floatEyeAI(game, p); break;                        // AI_002 飘浮眼（原版核）
        case 3:
          // AI_003 拜月教弓手 379/380 仪式待机（NPC.cs:56575-56601,ai[3]<0 静立面碑零伤；
          // 碑置 ai[3]=0 后激活复位 :56602-56609,回战士核）——bossAI_lunar_misc
          if ((this.vanillaId === 379 || this.vanillaId === 380) && this.ai3 < 0) {
            cultistArcherStandbyAI(this, game);
            break;
          }
          if (this.vanillaId === 379 || this.vanillaId === 380) cultistArcherWake(this);
          this.fighterAI(game, p);
          // 火把僵尸 590/591 手持火把发光（NPC.cs:57309-57366 AI_003 内 type 段）：
          //  590 按朝向取 (Center±22 / y+6) 格，火把格与相邻格均无液体才点亮；
          //  591 持械变体 !wet 即亮，偏移 ±36/±28（专家变体，尘需 ai[2]==0 门）。
          //  色 (1, 0.95, 0.8) = 与手持火把同色；尘 6 号 1/30 概率
          if (this.vanillaId === 590 || this.vanillaId === 591) this.torchZombieLight(game);
          // AI_003 火星尾段（bossAI_martian.ts）：军官 383 带盾无敌/补盾、工程师 386 放炮塔
          if (this.vanillaId === 383 || this.vanillaId === 386) martianFighterExtras(this, game, p);
          break;                                                        // AI_003 战士族（原版通用核）
        // EoC 传参例外:玩家死亡也要传原对象(cs:19931 dead 判定走逃离分支,
        // 冲刺计时合法冻结;传 null 会让 aiT 照走、状态机停摆 → 首冲时长翻倍)
        case 4: this.eocAI(game, (game as unknown as { player: Player }).player ?? null); break; // AI_004 克苏鲁之眼（NPC.cs:19909）
        case 5: this.swarmerAI(game, p); break;                         // AI_005 噬魂怪族（原版蜂群核）
        case 6: this.wormAI(game, p); break;                           // AI_006 蠕虫族（头）
        case 8: this.casterAI(game, p); break;                          // AI_008 法师族（传送+弹幕）
        case 10: this.cursedSkullAI(game, p); break;                     // AI_010 诅咒头骨（地牢飞行族）
        case 11:
          // 68 守卫走专用分支（bossAI.skeletronBossAI 注释"守卫分支已在 skeletronHeadAI"
          // 即此拆分：守卫恒速追击与骷髅王战斗状态机互不混用）
          if (this.vanillaId === 68) this.dungeonGuardianAI(game, p);
          else skeletronBossAI(this, game, p);
          break;                                                         // AI_011 骷髅王头战斗核/地牢守卫
        case 12: skeletronHandAI(this, game, p); break;                    // AI_012 骷髅王手(锚定头)
        case 20: this.blazingWheelAI(); break;                           // AI_020 火轮（地牢陷阱）
        case 21: this.spikeBallAI(game); break;                          // AI_021 刺球（地牢陷阱）

        case 31: twinsAI(this, game, p, true); break;                     // AI_031 魔神之眼 126(诅咒焰/火焰喷射)

        case 37: destroyerAI(this, game, p); break;                       // AI_037 毁灭者 134(80 段链穿地/探针激光)
        case 27: wallOfFleshAI(this, game, (game as unknown as { player: Player }).player ?? null); break; // AI_027 血肉墙(空腔扫描/速度血量档/Leech 轮)
        case 28: wofEyeAI(this, game, p); break;                          // AI_028 血肉墙之眼(钉墙吸附/激光连射 83)

// SetDefaults，原版由 DrawNPC case 402 自绘 trail；本仓蠕虫链 spawnWormChain 需要

// aiStyle 与 AI_XXX 方法的对应见 NPC.cs AI() 分发；此处先覆盖高频家族，
// 未移植 AI 家族的 aiStyle 回退到最接近的已移植行为（注释标注）。
import vanillaNpcsJson from '../../public/sprites/vanilla-npcs.json';
import vanillaDataJson from '../../public/sprites/vanilla.json';
import soundTracksJson from './vanilla-soundtracks.json';
import { ITEM_BY_KEY } from './items';
import { TILE_DEFS } from './tiles';

/** tile id → key 反查（biomeAt 群系判定用） */
const TILE_KEY_NAME: string[] = TILE_DEFS.map((d) => d.key);

// （旧掉落表已退役：掉落走 src/drops/NpcDrops.ts 规则树——
//  tools/extract-npcdrops.mjs 结构化提取 ItemDropDatabase.cs，含专家/大师/条件/链语义）

/** 原版物品 id → 本仓库 item key（vanilla.json 的 key 是 PascalCase，ITEM_BY_KEY 多为 snake_case；
 *  未注册的返回 null 跳过） */
export const vanillaItemKey = (() => {
  const map = new Map<number, string | null>();
  const items = (vanillaDataJson as unknown as { items: Record<string, { key?: string }> }).items ?? {};
  return (itemId: number): string | null => {
    if (map.has(itemId)) return map.get(itemId)!;
    const meta = items[String(itemId)];
    let key: string | null = null;
    if (meta?.key) {
      const snake = meta.key.replace(/([a-z0-9])([A-Z])/g, '$1_$2').toLowerCase();
      if (ITEM_BY_KEY[snake] != null) key = snake;
      else if (ITEM_BY_KEY[meta.key] != null) key = meta.key;
      // vi_NNN 导入物品（items.ts 的全量原版物品命名）：vi_<id> 或 vi_<id>_<snake>
      else if (ITEM_BY_KEY[`vi_${itemId}`] != null) key = `vi_${itemId}`;
      else if (ITEM_BY_KEY[`vi_${itemId}_${snake}`] != null) key = `vi_${itemId}_${snake}`;
    }
    map.set(itemId, key);
    return key;
  };
})();

export interface VanillaNpc {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  frames: number;
  lifeMax: number;
  damage: number;
  defense: number;
  knockBackResist: number;
  aiStyle: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  npcSlots: number;
  noGravity: boolean;
  noTileCollide: boolean;
  friendly: boolean;
  townNPC: boolean;
  HitSound: string;   // SoundID 名（NPCHitN / NPCDeathN）
  DeathSound: string;
  scale: number;
  /** SetDefaults alpha：每类型静态不透明度基线（渲染 1-alpha/255，NPC.Opacity；无通用渐隐） */
  alpha?: number;
  /** SetDefaults behindTiles：绘制在 tile 层之下（钻地族被前景方块遮挡，
   *  Main.cs:56457 DrawNPCs(behindTiles:true) 在实心 tile 之前绘制）。
   *  全表 46 id 取自 1456 SetDefaults（7-15/39-41/69/72/95-101/113-115/117-119/
   *  134-136/375/398/510-515/542-545/549/621-623）——飞龙 87/88 同为 aiStyle6 但
   *  非钻地不在表内 */
  behindTiles?: boolean;
  /** SetDefaults color：非 default 时 Main.DrawNPC 二次绘制同贴图（GetColor 逐像素乘法贴轮廓） */
  color?: number[];  // [r, g, b, a]
  critter?: boolean;  // NPCID.Sets.CountsAsCritter 小动物（tools/extract-critters.mjs 提取）
}

export const VANILLA_NPCS = vanillaNpcsJson as unknown as Record<string, VanillaNpc>;

/** GetGemSquirrelToSpawn（NPC.cs:5611-5623）/ GetGemBunnyToSpawn（:5591-5603）：
 *  纯加权随机表（Diamond 5%/Amber 8%/Ruby 10%/Emerald 12%/Sapphire 16%/Topaz 21%/Amethyst 28%）
 *  ——与就近宝石 tile、世界档位完全无关 */
export function getGemSquirrelToSpawn(rand: () => number): number {
  const num = Math.floor(rand() * 100);
  if (num < 5) return 644;
  if (num < 13) return 645;
  if (num < 23) return 643;
  if (num < 35) return 642;
  if (num < 51) return 641;
  if (num < 72) return 640;
  return 639;
}
export function getGemBunnyToSpawn(rand: () => number): number {
  const num = Math.floor(rand() * 100);
  if (num < 5) return 651;
  if (num < 13) return 652;
  if (num < 23) return 650;
  if (num < 35) return 649;
  if (num < 51) return 648;
  if (num < 72) return 647;
  return 646;
}

export function vanillaNpc(id: number): VanillaNpc | null {
  return VANILLA_NPCS[String(id)] ?? null;
}

// ================= 城镇 NPC（TownNPC 实体用） =================
// key → 原版 NPCID（Terarria1456/Terraria.ID/NPCID.cs:11099+）；

  private blazingWheelAI() {
    if (!this.aiInit) { this.aiInit = true;
      this.facing *= -1;
      this.y += this.h / 2 + 8;
      if (this.facing === 0) this.facing = 1;
      this.ai3 = 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 15) * 0.1;
      this.vy = 6 * this.ai3 * this.dirY;
      this.ai0 = 1;
      this.x += this.vx;
      this.y += this.vy;
      return;
    }
    const spd = 6 * this.ai3, accel = 0.2 * this.ai3;
    const phaseLen = Math.floor(spd / accel / 2); // num304（ai3 约掉=15）
    if (this.ai0 >= 1 && this.ai0 < phaseLen) {
      this.vy = this.dirY * spd;
      this.ai0 += 1;
    } else if (this.ai0 >= phaseLen) {
      this.vy = 0;
      this.dirY *= -1;
      this.vx = spd * this.facing;
      this.ai0 = -1;
    } else {
      // 常态弹跳（L24637-24660）：到 ±spd 翻向，持续加 accel
      if (this.dirY > 0 && this.vy >= spd) { this.dirY *= -1; this.vy = spd; }
      else if (this.dirY < 0 && this.vy <= -spd) { this.dirY *= -1; this.vy = -spd; }
      if (this.facing > 0 && this.vx >= spd) { this.facing *= -1; this.vx = spd; }
      else if (this.facing < 0 && this.vx <= -spd) { this.facing *= -1; this.vx = -spd; }
      this.vx += accel * this.facing;
      this.vy += accel * this.dirY;
    }
    this.x += this.vx;
    this.y += this.vy;
  }

// 实体与 Game 交互的接口（避免循环依赖，只放类型）
import type { World } from '../world/World';

export interface GameHooks {
  world: World;
  /** 主角(投射物命中判定用) */
  player: import('./Player').Player;
  /** 敌人列表(投射物命中判定用) */
  enemies(): unknown[];
  /** 城镇 NPC 自卫弹入投射物桶(attackUpdate 消费) */
  spawnTownShot?(shot: import('./TownShot').TownShot): void;
  /** 小动物列表(弹幕命中判定用;近战挥砍在 Game 内直接遍历桶) */
  critters(): unknown[];
  /** 城镇 NPC 列表(受击判定用;Game 实现 = entities.npcs 桶) */
  npcs?(): unknown[];
  /** 在世界上生成掉落物（返回实体便于染色等后处理） */
  spawnDrop(x: number, y: number, itemKey: string, count?: number, vx?: number, vy?: number): import('./ItemDrop').ItemDrop | null;
  /** 对玩家造成伤害（attacker 用于荆棘反伤/死亡归因；contactOnly=true 表示伤害源是
   *  NPC 接触（Player.cs:30880-30970 分支）——荆棘只在接触分支反伤，弹幕命中不触发；
   *  pvp=true 走 Hurt 的 PvP 语义：8t 无敌帧 + pvpDeath 标记） */
  damagePlayer(dmg: number, srcX: number, srcY: number, attacker?: unknown, contactOnly?: boolean, pvp?: boolean): void;

```ts
/** 近似 Main.mouseTextColor（Main.cs:18066-18073：190↔255 每帧 ±1，周期 130 帧） */
function mouseTextColorApprox(now = performance.now()): number {
  const t = Math.floor(now / 16.6667);
  const p = ((t % 130) + 130) % 130;
  // 上升段 65 帧 190→255，下降段 65 帧 255→190（对称三角波）
  return p < 65 ? 190 + p : 255 - (p - 65);
}
```

// ---- sparkle 彩虹（Main.hslToRgb，Main.cs:47266-47290 1:1）----
function hue2rgb(v1: number, v2: number, vH: number): number {
  if (vH < 0) vH += 1;
  if (vH > 1) vH -= 1;
  if (6 * vH < 1) return v1 + (v2 - v1) * 6 * vH;
  if (2 * vH < 1) return v2;
  if (3 * vH < 1) return v1 + (v2 - v1) * ((2 / 3) - vH) * 6;
  return v1;
}
/** Main.hslToRgb 1:1（GetShimmerGlitterColor :766-771 以 s=1/l=0.5 调用）→ RGB 0-1 */
function hslToRgb(hue: number, sat: number, lum: number): [number, number, number] {
  if (sat === 0) return [lum, lum, lum];
  const v2 = lum < 0.5 ? lum * (1 + sat) : lum + sat - lum * sat;
  const v1 = 2 * lum - v2;
  return [hue2rgb(v1, v2, hue + 1 / 3), hue2rgb(v1, v2, hue), hue2rgb(v1, v2, hue - 1 / 3)];
}

// ---- sparkle 染色变体缓存（离线预渲染）----
// 关键①：sparkle 闪纹是灰度像素（饱和度 0），CSS hue-rotate 对纯白/纯灰是 no-op——
// 旧实现 ctx.filter=hue-rotate 等于没上色，闪纹显示为白色而非原版彩虹。
// 故离线预渲染染色副本：hue 量化 16 档（((px+py/6)+t/30)/6 % 1），每档一条

  const n = Math.round(Number(v));
  if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return fallback;

/** HSL → RGB（0-1 输入/输出） */
export function hslToRgb(h: number, s: number, l: number): [number, number, number] {
  h = ((h % 1) + 1) % 1;
  const f = (n: number) => {
    const k = (n + h * 12) % 12;

  };
  return [f(0), f(8), f(4)];
}

export function rgbToHsl(r: number, g: number, b: number): [number, number, number] {
  const rr = r / 255, gg = g / 255, bb = b / 255;
  const max = Math.max(rr, gg, bb), min = Math.min(rr, gg, bb);
  const l = (max + min) / 2;
  let h = 0, s = 0;
  if (max !== min) {
    const d = max - min;

    else if (max === gg) h = ((bb - rr) / d + 2) / 6;
    else h = ((rr - gg) / d + 4) / 6;
  }
  return [h, s, l];
}

For 21276 (693): num116 = Remap(ai[0], 0, 60, 0, 1)². Utils.Remap(value, from1, from2, to1, to2) = clamp((value-from1)/(from2-from1),0,1)*(to2-to1)+to1. So num116 = clamp(ai0/60, 0, 1)². If ai0 < 60, num116 < 1... and > 0 whenever ai0 > 0. Since ai0 starts at 500 in this repo (vanilla: ai[0] init -1120? no — for caster it's 500? The repo sets ai0=500 per :20799-20801). Hmm wait — the light uses `Remap(this.ai[0], 0f, 60f, 0f, 1f)` — with ai[0] starting at 500, num116 = 1 → num116²=1 → full light (0.91,0.82,0.57) at Top. So for LibrarianSkeleton, the light is essentially always full after spawn (since ai[0] counts up from 500 and only resets to 1 on teleport, where it ramps 0→60 over 60 frames). So it's a "ramp up after teleport" effect. 

Position: `base.Top` → the light is at the top-center of the NPC: x = position.x + width/2, y = position.y. In lightRGBAt terms: {x: this.cx, y: this.y}.

For 21332 (533 DesertSpirit): Top, (0.6,0.6,0.3) — lightRGBAt {x: cx, y: y}.

For 21337 else: Center (0.6, 0.39, 0.24) — which types? 24 FireImp, 29 GoblinSorcerer, 32 DarkCaster, 45 Tim, 281-286. (693, 533, 172 have their own branches; 172 RuneWizard → the type==172 branch has only dust, NO light! So RuneWizard gets NO light. Correct — in vanilla, Rune Wizard doesn't emit light.)

So:
```ts
// ---- 点光（cs:21274-21340 粉尘段后的 AddLight 链）----
if (vid === 693) {
  // 693 图书管理员骷髅：Remap(ai[0],0,60,0,1)² 渐亮（传送后 0→60 帧 0→1），位置 Top
  const ramp = Math.min(Math.max(this.ai0 / 60, 0), 1);
  const n = ramp * ramp;
  if (n > 0) { this.lightRGB = [0.91 * n, 0.82 * n, 0.57 * n]; this.lightRGBAt = { x: this.cx, y: this.y }; }
} else if (vid === 533) {
  this.lightRGB = [0.6, 0.6, 0.3]; this.lightRGBAt = { x: this.cx, y: this.y };
} else if (vid !== 172) {
  this.lightRGB = [0.6, 0.39, 0.24];
}
```

Wait — for the else branch: vanilla colors are num123=0.6, 0.6*0.65=0.39, 0.6*0.4=0.24. Yes.

Note: `base.Top` in XNA = position + (width/2, 0). AddLight(Vector2) → (int)X/16, (int)Y/16. So tile = (cx/16, y/16). Using lightRGBAt with pixel coords {x: this.cx, y: this.y} works since Game.ts presumably does floor(at.x/TILE).

Also note: casterAI has `if (!player) return;` before the attack logic — the light must be placed BEFORE that return to run every tick. I'll insert the light right after the movement (after `this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;`) and before `if (!player) return;`. Good — that's also semantically right (vanilla light code is after movement, part of the visual section).

The direction in vanilla = `direction` (facing). Note `(int)(position.X + width/2 + direction*(width+8))/16` — operator precedence: (int)(...) applies to the sum then /16. So tile x = floor((position.x + width/2 + direction*(width+8))/16).

 "new_string": "/** 无 key 映射的原版物品 id（一次性告警用） */\nconst UNMAPPED_WARNED = new Set<number>();\n\n// ================= NPC 侧点光的共享近似源（GameHooks 取不到 FlickerClock/Main 计数器） =========\n/** 近似 Main.timeForVisualEffects（Main.cs:17110-17113：每帧 +1、216000 回卷）。\n *  以 performance.now() 折帧数（16.6667ms/帧）——光色波（hsl 时间色/WrappedLerp）\n *  只需视觉节拍，帧漂移无感 */\nexport function visEffectFrames(): number {\n  return (performance.now() / 16.6667) % 216000;\n}\n/** 近似 Main.mouseTextColor（Main.cs:18066-18073：190↔255 每帧 ±1 三角波，周期 130 帧）。\n *  消费点：cs:24722（火轮 num306=(270−mouseTextColor)/400）、cs:93547 等\n *  UpdateNPC_CastLights 的 (270−mouseTextColor)/500 抖动项 */\nexport function mouseTextColorApprox(): number {\n  const t = visEffectFrames();\n  const p = t % 130;\n  return p < 65 ? 190 + p : 255 - (p - 65);\n}"
}

I approximated with visEffectFrames/54000 * 0.33. visEffectFrames is frames since page load (performance.now based), not game time — but it advances at the same rate as game frames (1 frame per tick at 60fps), and GlobalTimeWrappedHourly advances 1/54000 per frame. So (frames/54000) equals GlobalTimeWrappedHourly's rate (mod the 24-hour wrap, irrelevant since ×0.33 % 1 only needs the fractional). Actually careful: GlobalTimeWrappedHourly wraps at 24, so at wrap the hue jumps — with frames/54000 there's no wrap and hue is continuous; the %1 in my hslToRgb handles it. Actually vanilla: `GlobalTimeWrappedHourly * 0.33f % 1f` — the wrap at 24h introduces a discontinuity in the hue (24*0.33 = 7.92 → wraps differently). Minor. Fine.

essScale oscillates 0.7 ↔ 1.0 (step 0.01/frame), so (essScale+0.5)/2 ∈ [0.6, 0.75]. The repo's approximation of a flat 0.5 is a bit low (should be ~0.6-0.75). This is a pre-existing approximation in a site the task listed as done ("glow snail / lava snail :34625/:34629" done — but 34496 nightcrawler wasn't explicitly in the done list; the done list mentioned "firefly family, glow snail/lava snail, bossAI_martian two places, Medusa, 590/591, Arrow 985/502, Dart all removed").

Since I'm auditing all sites, I should improve this one to match the vanilla range. The oscillation is a slow triangle wave (0.7→1.0→0.7, 30+30 = 60 frames). I'll use a triangle approximation via mouseTextColorApprox-like helper on visEffectFrames.

Let me update the 484 light to use the proper range:
```ts
if (this.vanillaId === 484) {
  // cs:34492-34497: num1047 = Next(90,111)×0.01 × (essScale+0.5)/2
  //   essScale: Main.cs:61703-61713 0.7↔1.0 triangular wave with step 0.01/frame (60-frame cycle)
  const t = visEffectFrames() % 60;
  const ess = (t < 30 ? 1 - t / 100 : 0.7 + (t - 30) / 100);
  const f = (0.9 + Math.random() * 0.2) * (ess + 0.5) / 2;
  this.lightRGB = [0.3 * f, 0.1 * f, 0.25 * f];
}
```
Check the triangle: phase A (30 frames): 1.0 → 0.7 (step -0.01): ess = 1 - 0.01*t for t in [0,30]. Phase B: 0.7 → 1.0: ess = 0.7 + 0.01*(t-30). Yes, my formula: t<30 → 1 - t/100 = 1-0.01t ✓; else 0.7 + (t-30)/100 ✓.

Let me apply this improvement (it's within my audit mandate — the site was implemented with a wrong constant).

52:function solidTileAt(game: GameHooks, x: number, y: number): boolean {
function solidTileAt(game: GameHooks, x: number, y: number): boolean {
  const st = game.world.store;
  const tx = Math.floor(x / TILE), ty = Math.floor(y / TILE);
  return st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty);
}
/** NPC.AnyNPCs(type)：指定 vanillaId 的存活 NPC 是否在场（弹跳球族 FTW 门用） */
function anyNpcs(game: GameHooks, id: number): boolean {
  return (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).some((n) => !n.dead && n.vanillaId === id);
}

export function hungryAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
  // :26299-26301 justHit（受击单帧旗标，hurt() 置位）→ ai[1]=10（脱落 10t 后回挂墙态）
  if (e.justHit) e.ai1 = 10;
  const head = headOf(e, game);
  // :26303-26306 Main.wofNPCIndex<0(墙已不在)→ 自毁
  if (!head) { e.dead = true; return; }
  // ai0 初值 -1120(史莱姆语义)复位:非墙生成的 Hungry 挂空腔中点
  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; if (e.ai0 < -1000) e.ai0 = 0.5; }

  // :26308-26335 挂墙半径/伤害/防御随墙血量变化；<50%：伤害 60 防 30（经典半径 700，
  //   专家半径不变但步进 num414+0.066）；<75%：伤害 45 防 20（经典半径 500 / 专家 +0.033）
  //   ——注意半径改值被【经典档门】包住，专家档走步进加成
  const expert = mainExpertMode(game.world);   // Main.expertMode（Main.cs:2762）
  let num414 = 0.1;   // 步进
  let num415 = 300;   // 挂墙半径
  const b = baseOf(e);
  e.def.damage = b.dmg; // :26311 damage = defDamage(每 tick 复位)
  let num416 = 0;
  if (head.hp < head.maxHp * 0.5) {
    num416 = 60;
    e.def.defense = 30;
    if (!expert) num415 = 700;
    else num414 += 0.066;

mourningWoodAI already has the light at line 137-138! It says "点光(:32958-32965)：底部 -30px" and sets lightRGB but NOT lightRGBAt — the comment claims "底部 -30px" position but the implementation doesn't set lightRGBAt. In vanilla the position is `base.Bottom + new Vector2(0,-30)` = (position.x + width/2, position.y + height - 30). So the repo is missing the offset. This is a bug worth fixing (audit finding): add lightRGBAt = {x: e.cx, y: e.y + e.h - 30}.

Let me fix it.

function queenTeleportSpot(game: GameHooks, player: Player, anti: boolean): { x: number; y: number; found: boolean } {
  if (anti) return { x: player.x + player.w / 2, y: player.y + player.h, found: false }; // :43406-43409
  const st = game.world.store;
  const ptx = Math.floor(player.cx / TILE), pty = Math.floor(player.cy / TILE);
  const cands: Array<[number, number]> = [];
  for (let x = ptx - 10; x <= ptx + 10; x++) {
    for (let y = pty - 10; y <= pty + 10; y++) {
      if (!st.inBounds(x, y) || !st.isSolid(x, y)) continue;
      let clear = true;
      for (let k = 1; k <= 7; k++) {                       // 头顶 7 格(BuildCache 10/7 参数)
        if (st.inBounds(x, y - k) && st.isSolid(x, y - k)) { clear = false; break; }
      }
      if (clear) cands.push([x, y]);
    }
  }
  if (cands.length > 0) {
    const [x, y] = cands[Math.floor(Math.random() * cands.length)];
    return { x: x * TILE + TILE / 2, y: y * TILE, found: true };
  }
  return { x: player.x + player.w / 2, y: player.y + player.h, found: false }; // :43430-43433 兜底
}

      if (c.nightOnly && isDay) return false;
      // 鸭子是两栖：水面列可生成（落在岸边浅水/滩涂），其余地面种不能在水列

      if (c.oceanOnly && !inOceanBand) return false;

    const total = pool.reduce((s2, c) => s2 + c.weight, 0);
    let r = Math.random() * total;
    let pick = pool[0];
    for (const c of pool) { r -= c.weight; if (r <= 0) { pick = c; break; } }

    if (pick.kind === 'fly') {

    } else if (pick.water && surfaceWater) {
      // 水生种（鸭子）：落在水面上（自上而下第一个液体格），不是湖底
      let surfY = 4;
      while (surfY < st.h - 4 && st.liquid[st.idx(tx, surfY)] <= 80) surfY++;

      this.entities.add(new Critter(pick.key, tx * TILE + 8, surfY * TILE), 'critters');

```
  static spawnWormChain(head: Enemy, segCount: number): Enemy[] {
    const segs: Enemy[] = [];
    const bodyId = head.vanillaId! + 1, tailId = head.vanillaId! + 2;
    // EoW 族记链总段数（血条恒定分母,EaterOfWorldsProgressBar :30-33;
    // 毁灭者族不消费,统一写入无副作用）
    const chainTotal = segCount + 1;
    head.eowChainMax = chainTotal;
    let prev = head;
    for (let k = 0; k < segCount; k++) {
      const id = k === segCount - 1 ? tailId : bodyId;
      const s = Enemy.fromVanilla(id, head.cx, head.cy);
      if (!s) continue;
      s.wormFollow = prev;
      prev.wormNext = s;
      prev = s;
      s.eowChainMax = chainTotal;
      segs.push(s);
    }
    return segs;
  }


  /** 用原版数据造怪：属性/碰撞/音效全部来自 SetDefaults 提取值 */
  static fromVanilla(id: number, x: number, y: number): Enemy | null {
    const v = vanillaNpc(id);
    if (!v) return null;
    const e = new Enemy(`v_${id}`, x, y);
    e.vanillaId = id;
    e.vanilla = v;
    const hit = vanillaSoundFiles(v.HitSound) ?? ['NPC_Hit_1'];
    const kill = vanillaSoundFiles(v.DeathSound) ?? ['NPC_Killed_1'];
    const flying = v.noGravity || v.aiStyle === 2 || v.aiStyle === 5 || v.aiStyle === 14;
    // ---- NPC.ScaleStats（NPC.cs:18081-18105，NewNPC→SetDefaults 后调用 :8322/:17791）----
    // 世界难度轴（Main.Difficulty → 生命/伤害/钱/击退倍率 + 逐类型系数 +
    // 专家困难模式兜底增强）。Boss **不豁免**（仅 ExpertHardmode 段跳过 :18471）。
    // 上下文经 bindScaleStatsWorld 注入（Game.afterWorldLoad；原版 Main 静态单例语义），
    // 未绑定=经典档（倍率恒 1，仅 lifeMax<6 下限与类型系数在 Classic 档同样无变化）。
    const rawDamage = (v.critter || v.friendly) ? 0 : v.damage;   // def.damage 基线
    // 原版"承受比例"语义——缺省 1f（NPC.cs:8449 SetDefaults 默认 knockBackResist=1,
    // JSON 提取表只写显式赋值,无字段的 137 只（克眼仆从等）此前落 0.5 吃半击退,2026-08-13 修正）
    const rawKb = v.knockBackResist ?? 1;

    // zenith(getfixedboi)→getGood(FTW)→tenthAnniversary（else-if 互斥）。命中分支

    const seedS: SeedAdjustStats = {
      type: id, scale: v.scale ?? 1, lifeMax: v.lifeMax, defense: v.defense, damage: v.damage,

	public static float Remap(float fromValue, float fromMin, float fromMax, float toMin, float toMax, bool clamped = true)
	{
		return MathHelper.Lerp(toMin, toMax, GetLerpValue(fromMin, fromMax, fromValue, clamped));
	}
	public static float GetLerpValue(float from, float to, float t, bool clamped = false)
	{
		if (clamped)
		{
			if (from < to)
			{
				if (t < from)
				{
					return 0f;
				}
				if (t > to)
				{
					return 1f;
				}
			}

### 复审做了什么

1. **跨文件扫描**：`Terraria/` 全目录搜 `Lighting.AddLight`——NPC 侧只有 `NPC.cs` 一个文件（80 处）。`Main.cs` 的 5 处全是**弹幕**驱动（proj.type 646-649/488），Dust/Gore/WorldItem/Mount/Player/Projectile 属其他实体族（掉落物/弹幕光已有独立批次与 Game.ts 扫描循环），均非 NPC 侧。任务范围外零漏。
2. **程序化对账**：把 80 行调用点逐行分类——**新实装 36 + debuff 光 7 + 既有已对号 29（含 4 处修正）+ 登记跳过 8 = 80，闭合无未分类**。
3. **分发可达性核查**：所有带光小动物（653/661/484/583-585）json `critter=true` → 确认走 `critterWanderAI` 家族分派（butterflyAI/wormCritterAI/fairyAI），光代码真实可达。

### 复审新发现并已修复：毁灭者身/尾段漏光

原版 AI_037 对头 134/身 135/尾 136 **每段独立**跑 buried 扫描+发光；本仓身/尾是 `wormFollow` 链（`wormBody`）跳过 AI 分发，之前我把光写进 `destroyerAI`，结果只有头发光。
修复：`bossAI.ts` 新导出 `destroyerSegmentLight(e, game)`（原版 :50278-50306 的 AABB 外扩 1-2 格嵌实心/液体扫描 + `!buried && !(135&&ai2==1)` 门 + `[0.3,0.1,0.05]`），在 Enemy 共享尾段对全族三 id 统一调用——头与段都恰好写一次，无双写。
